PHASE 192 ENUMERATION OF REMAINING TRACKED BARE-SLUG MATCHES (D-01)
====================================================================
capture_date: 2026-09-14T04:55:47Z
capture_commit: 90321cf2581e86a56ed37a3f03a4f9ccf10aa63c

This is D-01's one-time enumeration of every remaining tracked bare-slug
match in the meta repository, taken after Tasks 1 and 2 of this plan are
committed, so README.md and the two runnable v1.4 artefacts already read
clean. It exists so the allowlist's blast radius -- everything this phase
deliberately does NOT edit -- is a recorded decision rather than an
executor's silence (D-01).

The enumeration is taken in two steps so that no fallible `git` sits in a
non-final pipeline stage, where its exit status would be swallowed by the
last stage and a broken scan would read as clean. First the tracked file
list is materialised and its own git exit status checked; then a
NUL-delimited loop reads that file, so a path containing whitespace cannot
truncate the scan. The filename prefix is forced because a single-file
grep omits it, and paths therefore appear absolute in the output below,
recorded exactly as emitted.

tracked_files_visited: 5074
reading_1_total: 948
reading_2_control_total: 1595
edited_subset: 10 -- README.md, .planning/v1.4-e2e-verify.sh, .planning/v1.4-RELEASE-PROCEDURES.md, .planning/codebase/ARCHITECTURE.md, .planning/codebase/CONCERNS.md, .planning/codebase/CONVENTIONS.md, .planning/codebase/INTEGRATIONS.md, .planning/codebase/STACK.md, .planning/codebase/STRUCTURE.md, .planning/codebase/TESTING.md

reading_2_control_total is non-zero (1595, pattern henols/firestarter_prom
over the same tracked-file set), which is what makes reading_1_total's
count non-vacuous: the same loop, the same git ls-files machinery, over
the same commit still finds a huge number of firestarter_prom references,
so the 948 bare-slug matches below are a property of the bare slug
specifically, not an artefact of an empty or broken scan.

tracked_files_visited (5074) equals the line count of `git ls-files`
written to a file and counted separately (also 5074, lsfiles_rc=0),
proving the NUL-delimited loop visited every tracked file rather than
stopping early or silently truncating on a path containing whitespace.

Of README.md, .planning/v1.4-e2e-verify.sh and .planning/v1.4-RELEASE-PROCEDURES.md
in the edited_subset above, none appears in the match list below: Tasks 1
and 2 of this plan already repointed every bare-slug site in those three
files, so their zero bare-slug count is confirmed by absence from this
list rather than merely asserted. The seven .planning/codebase/ documents
are owned by plans 02 and 03 of this phase and have not yet been remapped
at the time this enumeration was taken -- their current bare-slug matches
below are prefixed EDITED because they are inside this phase's edited
subset, even though the remap that clears them runs in a later plan.

Full match list follows, in the NUL-delimited loop's own emission order.
Each entry is prefixed EDITED when its path is in the edited_subset above,
or prefixed RECORD when it is not:

RECORD /workspaces/.planning/ROADMAP.md:30:- ✅ **v1.34 Pre-Merge Hardware Regression Validation** — Phases 160–166 (**CLOSED 2026-08-29 — EARLY / SCOPE-REDUCED by operator direction, not a completed sweep**; originally **ACTIVATED 2026-08-25**; bench-evidence milestone, no feature work — project-level research deliberately skipped at activation because v1.34 adds nothing to research. Phase numbering continues at **160**; the vacated **150** slot and the v1.24–v1.29 version slots stay unreused so every by-number cross-reference keeps resolving). **Prove on real silicon that v1.33's −2938 B flash / −13 B RAM size reduction changed nothing behavioural, before [`prom#43`](https://github.com/henols/firestarter_prom/pull/43) / [`fw#56`](https://github.com/henols/firestarter/pull/56) / [`app#54`](https://github.com/henols/firestarter_app/pull/54) merge to `beta`.** v1.33 closed locally on a premise of byte-level equivalence — heap allocator removed, the 438 B 64-bit runtime dropped, `jsmntok_t` narrowed 8 → 6 B, `key_parsers[]` rewritten, handle types narrowed — every claim backed by native tests, golden traces and cold builds, and **not one of them run on an Arduino.** **Five cells × two arms × two chips = 20 full write→read→verify cycles:** A1 Uno/Rev 2.0, A2 uno328pb/Rev 2.0, A3/B2 Leonardo/Rev 2.0 (the v1.31 reference rig — **executed once in Phase 161 and cited by Phase 163**, never re-run), B1 Leonardo/Modified Rev 0, B3 Leonardo/Rev 2.2; chips **W27C512** (`0x07`, 65536 B) and **W29C020** (`0x05`, 262144 B page-write). **The control arm is the deliverable, not a formality** — firmware `8695ee5` / host `6bfa645`, the exact merge-bases the v1.33 branches forked from; without it "this failed" cannot be distinguished from "this has always failed here," which is precisely the comparison v1.31 declined to make. Oracle is **full-device read-back SHA equality, never an exit code**, with N=3 read stability on the v1.33 arm. Plus an 11-part `dev test` sweep of the v1.15 inventory on the rig Phase 161 leaves standing (no reconfiguration, no re-flash — the phase order is chosen for rig economy, since every reconfiguration costs a re-verified `controller:` identity and a re-declared shield revision), the first sweep of the v1.7 A3 ADC shield-detection plumbing across all three physical shields, and the long-blocked **Modified Rev 0 rework trace** (photographs, cut/jumper trace against schematic blob `d2a7f691`, and the `TBD pending Phase 35` cells in `v1.7-SHIELD-REVS.md` §4/§5 filled or each given a named reason). **Known faults declared BEFORE the bench runs** so a red cell is never misread as a v1.33 break: uno328pb cannot finish a program (Backlog **999.2**), **W27E512** @0x3d and **W27E040** @0x7db carry D-32 stuck erase bits, **W29C040**'s §6.6 boot block is permanently locked making a full-device verify physically impossible (**CR-01**), **AM27C020** is marginal not deterministic (write#1 60/64, write#2 0/64) and can arbitrate nothing. **Only v1.33-caused regressions are fixed in-milestone, and their fixes land on the v1.33 PR branch — not on v1.34's own branch — so the open PR ships fixed.** **v1.34 performs NO merge, no push to `beta`, no sub-repo tag, no beta cut and no release** — operator-gated precedent unbroken since v1.21, and a merge to `beta` auto-fires a pre-release cut. Closes with a signed-off evidence table, an explicit merge / merge-with-caveats / do-not-merge recommendation naming the rows it rests on, and an honesty ledger whose headline non-claim is that program-window VPP/VCC **under load** stays unmeasured (the Phase-97 DTR-reset-on-close tooling gap), so **v1.34 makes no electrical claim**. Three seeds triggered at activation and all three were declined to keep this a regression gate rather than a feature milestone: white-box voltage calibration, the Rev 2.2 3-pin header + 2516-family support, and the per-pin-map jumper table.

RECORD /workspaces/.planning/ROADMAP.md:5792:**Triage note (2026-09-13, from `/gsd-explore`):** Impact measured against the live repos and the GitHub/PyPI APIs — full analysis in [`notes/999.9-repo-rename-impact-analysis.md`](notes/999.9-repo-rename-impact-analysis.md); the destructive half is deferred as [`seeds/SEED-claim-firestarter-slug.md`](seeds/SEED-claim-firestarter-slug.md) and its gate is an open research question. **Four corrections to the goal above.** (1) **The stated validation is blind.** A clean-environment `pip install firestarter` resolves to **2.0.7** — the whole `3.0.0bNN` line is prerelease — so "full clean-environment install → … → update-check" exercises `origin/main` (948 commits behind `beta`, no `submit.py`, but carrying `FIRESTARTER_RELEASE_URL`), not the branch being fixed. Repointing `beta` alone would pass that validation with the default install still broken. `main` and `beta` are separate changes, and reaching today's default installs additionally requires cutting a **stable** off the 2.0.7 line. (2) **Only the *claim* is destructive.** Both renames are individually covered by permanent redirects — `firestarter_prom`'s survives forever, so `SUBMIT_REPO` keeps working on old CLIs indefinitely. Re-occupying `henols/firestarter` is what deletes the firmware redirect; it should be gated on stable adoption, not on a date, and is the deferred seed. (3) **"CI/release workflows" is a no-op** — no workflow in any of the three repos hardcodes a slug, and the meta repo has no `.github/workflows/` at all (`.planning/codebase/STACK.md:140-150` describes a cross-repo catalog-sync workflow that no longer exists). (4) **The `.gitmodules` trap cannot be fixed, only documented** — history keeps the old URL, so any pre-rename checkout plus `submodule update --init` clones the meta repo into `firestarter/`. **Scope is narrower than "high blast radius" suggests:** the three hardcoded URLs are consumed only by `firmware.py`, so a stranded CLI loses `fw` alone — read/write/verify/erase/`dev test` are untouched. **Standing rule this item must carry:** never publish a GitHub Release on the meta repo (it has **0** today) — one Release arms `_compare_versions` to read `v1.36` as `1.36`, judge `3.0.0b28` newer, and report firmware as current forever, turning a clean 404 into a silent permanent wrong answer. **Sweep scope:** 778 firmware-slug references in meta, but **672 are archived under `.planning/milestones/`** and must **not** be repaired — `.planning/`→`.planning/` citations are historical-by-intent; the live tracked surface is ~12 files.

**999.24** (gh#17) follows — the native CMake + `arm-none-eabi-gcc` target itself (no Arduino framework, no RTOS), `platform/py32f071/` tree, one-snapshot `IDR` reads + atomic `BSRR` writes, CherryUSB CDC, SysTick/hardware-timer timing, VREFINT-compensated 12-bit ADC, on-chip 12-bit DAC closed-loop VPP with independent overvoltage shutdown, CRC-validated dual-slot flash config (the part has no EEPROM). **Hard acceptance constraint:** Uno, ATmega328PB, Leonardo and the native test suite must remain unaffected. **Sequence AFTER v1.26** — 999.23's VREFINT + two-point calibration is the *same* model v1.26 designs for AVR, so v1.26 must define it once and this milestone extends it cross-platform rather than inventing a second one; likewise 999.24's closed-loop DAC VPP is a genuine capability change that would supersede part of v1.26's hand-set-pot procedure (`feedback_operator_adjusts_pot_solo`). **Prior art — verified 2026-07-27:** [`henols/firestarter` PR #46](https://github.com/henols/firestarter/pull/46) ("Add native PY32F071 HAL, backend plan, and firmware toolchain") was **CLOSED unmerged as a draft on 2026-07-21**, so this work is *not* in flight — but the branch `feature/py32f071-toolchain` survives at `2c2ed10` with 603 additions across 8 files, including a 195-line `platform/py32f071/PORTING.md` stating the combined HAL + native-backend contract, plus an ARM GCC CMake toolchain, PY32F071xB linker script, Puya-derived vector table and a CI workflow emitting ELF/BIN/HEX + SHA256SUMS. **Start scoping from that branch, not from scratch.** Bench cost is the main risk: a fourth target multiplies every board-gated procedure this project runs. Issues: [gh#16](https://github.com/henols/firestarter_prom/issues/16) · [gh#17](https://github.com/henols/firestarter_prom/issues/17).

Closes with a signed-off evidence table, an explicit merge / merge-with-caveats / do-not-merge recommendation naming the rows it rests on, and an honesty ledger whose headline non-claim is that program-window VPP/VCC **under load** stays unmeasured (the Phase-97 DTR-reset-on-close tooling gap), so **v1.34 makes no electrical claim**. RECORD /workspaces/.planning/milestones/v1.4-REQUIREMENTS.md:51:- [x] **INST-02**: `firestarter --install --pre` fetches the newest *pre-release* firmware for the configured board. Selection algorithm: list all releases via `/repos/henols/firestarter/releases` (paginated if needed), filter to `prerelease: true`, sort by PEP 440 version order, pick the highest, then resolve the asset `browser_download_url` for the matching board (Uno, Leonardo, or other configured board) — same asset-resolution logic as INST-01. RECORD /workspaces/.planning/milestones/v1.4-REQUIREMENTS.md:52:- [x] **INST-03**: `firestarter --install --firmware-version X.Y.Z[bN|rcN]` fetches the exact firmware version from `/repos/henols/firestarter/releases/tags/{tag}`. Works for both stable (`3.1.0`) and pre-release (`3.1.0b2`) tags. Tag input is validated against the PEP 440 regex used in Phase 15's `BETA_VERSION_RE`; invalid input fails fast with a clear error and no network call. Mutually exclusive with `--pre`.
RECORD /workspaces/.planning/milestones/v1.4-ROADMAP.md:85:  1. **INST-01 (stable non-regression):** `firestarter --install` invoked with no new flags on a stable-installed app hits `api.github.com/repos/henols/firestarter/releases/latest`, parses `tag_name` + the matching board's `browser_download_url`, downloads the `.hex`, and proceeds to flash. Behavior is byte-identical to the pre-v1.4 download URL + asset selection — verified by a pytest fixture that compares HTTP-request shape and post-download file path against a captured baseline. The `_compare_versions` refactor is invisible at the URL level (only the internal comparison shape changes; PEP 440 `X.Y.Z` parses identically to today's int-tuple).
RECORD /workspaces/.planning/milestones/v1.4-ROADMAP.md:86:  2. **INST-02 (`--pre`):** `firestarter --install --pre` hits `/repos/henols/firestarter/releases` (paginated), filters to `prerelease: true`, sorts by PEP 440 version order, picks the highest, resolves the board-matching asset, downloads + flashes. If no pre-release exists, falls back to stable (mirrors `pip install --pre`). RECORD /workspaces/.planning/milestones/v1.4-phases/18-beta-aware-firmware-downloader/18-RESEARCH.md:78:The second important finding is the GitHub API shape. RECORD /workspaces/.planning/v1.5-BENCH-RESULTS.md:6:**Firmware shipped to chip:** `firestarter_uno328pb.hex` v3.0.0b4 (from GitHub Pre-release `henols/firestarter` tag `3.0.0b4`)

What this enumeration deliberately does NOT cover
-----------------------------------------------------
D-01's rule: edit only what a reader could act on today; everything else
under `.planning/` is a record of what the repository was called when the
record was written, and repairing it destroys that evidence. Excluded
classes: `.planning/milestones/` under D-5; the record files under
`.planning/phases/`, `.planning/notes/`, `.planning/seeds/`,
`.planning/todos/`, `.planning/debug/` and `.planning/v1.3x/`; and
`.planning/PROJECT.md`.

The total above will keep growing while this phase runs, because this
phase's own planning records -- this plan's PLAN.md, CONTEXT.md,
PATTERNS.md, and STATE.md's session log -- name the old slug in describing
the work. That is expected, not a regression: this phase's own working
records are themselves records the moment they are written, and D-01
applies to them exactly as it applies to any other historical record.
